python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
create_v3_toolkit.py
Writes all 16 files of the Sonic Delta 40Mb M68K v3 Reverse-Engineering Toolkit
and packages them into sonic_delta_m68k_toolkit_v3.zip.
"""

import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

FILES = {}

FILES["import_upstream_constants.py"] = '''#!/usr/bin/env python3
import re
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

CONSTANT_FILES = [
    ("s1", "s1disasm-AS/_Constants.asm"),
    ("s1", "s1disasm-AS/_Variables.asm"),
    ("s2", "s2disasm-master/s2.constants.asm"),
    ("s3", "skdisasm-master/s3.constants.asm"),
    ("sk", "skdisasm-master/sk.constants.asm"),
]

DEF_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\\s*([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\\s*(?:=|equ|EQU)\\s*\\$([0-9A-Fa-f]{4,8})\\b"
)

def normalize_68k_ram_addr(val):
    addr = val & 0x00FFFFFF
    if 0x8000 <= val <= 0xFFFF:
        addr = 0x00FF0000 | val
    return addr

def is_valid_hardware_or_ram(addr):
    return (
        (0x00FF0000 <= addr <= 0x00FFFFFF)
        or (0x00A00000 <= addr <= 0x00A01FFF)
        or (0x00A10000 <= addr <= 0x00C0001F)
    )

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Extract upstream RAM & hardware symbols")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default="upstream_ram_symbols.json", help="Output JSON path")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    symbols_by_addr = {}
    parsed_files = 0

    for prefix, rel_path in CONSTANT_FILES:
        p = Path(rel_path)
        if not p.exists():
            print(f"[!] Skipping missing file: {p}")
            continue

        parsed_files += 1
        text = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
        for line in text.splitlines():
            code_part = line.split(";")[0]
            m = DEF_RE.match(code_part)
            if not m:
                continue
            name = m.group(1)
            raw_val = int(m.group(2), 16)
            addr = normalize_68k_ram_addr(raw_val)
            if not is_valid_hardware_or_ram(addr):
                continue

            addr_key = f"0x{addr:06X}"
            entry = symbols_by_addr.setdefault(addr_key, {"address_int": addr, "names": []})
            tagged_name = f"{prefix}_{name}"
            if tagged_name not in entry["names"]:
                entry["names"].append(tagged_name)

    Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(symbols_by_addr, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[+] Parsed {parsed_files} constant files -> {len(symbols_by_addr)} unique addresses saved to {args.out}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
'''

FILES["extract_neto_custom.py"] = '''#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json
import struct
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

BANK_SIZE = 0x80000

SSF2_REGS = {
    0x00A130F1: "SSF2_SRAM_CTRL",
    0x00A130F3: "SSF2_BANK_1",
    0x00A130F5: "SSF2_BANK_2",
    0x00A130F7: "SSF2_BANK_3",
    0x00A130F9: "SSF2_BANK_4",
    0x00A130FB: "SSF2_BANK_5",
    0x00A130FD: "SSF2_BANK_6",
    0x00A130FF: "SSF2_BANK_7",
}

NETO_SIGNATURES = [
    b"Neto - Depurador",
    b"Super Depurador",
    b"Mapeador n",
    b"SRAM n",
    b"Sonic Delta Next",
    b"SONIC DELTA 40Mb",
    b"M68K Bank",
    b"neto.baiano",
]

def decode_simple_m68k(rom, offset):
    if offset + 2 > len(rom):
        return "dc.w $0000", 2

    op = struct.unpack(">H", rom[offset : offset + 2])[0]

    if op == 0x13FC and offset + 8 <= len(rom):
        imm = struct.unpack(">H", rom[offset + 2 : offset + 4])[0] & 0xFF
        addr = struct.unpack(">I", rom[offset + 4 : offset + 8])[0]
        reg_name = SSF2_REGS.get(addr, f"${addr:08X}")
        return f"move.b  #${imm:02X}, ({reg_name}).l", 8

    if (op & 0xFFF8) == 0x13C0 and offset + 6 <= len(rom):
        dn = op & 0x0007
        addr = struct.unpack(">I", rom[offset + 2 : offset + 6])[0]
        reg_name = SSF2_REGS.get(addr, f"${addr:08X}")
        return f"move.b  d{dn}, ({reg_name}).l", 6

    if op == 0x4A39 and offset + 6 <= len(rom):
        addr = struct.unpack(">I", rom[offset + 2 : offset + 6])[0]
        reg_name = SSF2_REGS.get(addr, f"${addr:08X}")
        return f"tst.b   ({reg_name}).l", 6

    if op == 0x4EB9 and offset + 6 <= len(rom):
        addr = struct.unpack(">I", rom[offset + 2 : offset + 6])[0]
        return f"jsr     (${addr:08X}).l", 6

    if op == 0x4EF9 and offset + 6 <= len(rom):
        addr = struct.unpack(">I", rom[offset + 2 : offset + 6])[0]
        return f"jmp     (${addr:08X}).l", 6

    if op == 0x4E75:
        return "rts", 2
    if op == 0x4E73:
        return "rte", 2

    return f"dc.w    ${op:04X}", 2

def scan_ssf2_accesses(rom):
    hits = []
    n = len(rom)
    for pos in range(0, n - 4, 2):
        val = struct.unpack(">I", rom[pos : pos + 4])[0]
        if val in SSF2_REGS:
            reg_name = SSF2_REGS[val]
            inst_offset = pos - 2
            if pos >= 4:
                prev2 = struct.unpack(">H", rom[pos - 4 : pos - 2])[0]
                if prev2 == 0x13FC:
                    inst_offset = pos - 4

            disasm, size = decode_simple_m68k(rom, inst_offset)
            hits.append({
                "rom_offset": f"0x{inst_offset:06X}",
                "rom_offset_int": inst_offset,
                "bank": inst_offset // BANK_SIZE,
                "bank_offset": f"0x{inst_offset % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
                "register": f"0x{val:06X}",
                "register_name": reg_name,
                "disassembly": disasm,
                "bytes": rom[inst_offset : inst_offset + size].hex().upper(),
            })
    return hits

def scan_neto_strings(rom):
    found = []
    for sig in NETO_SIGNATURES:
        start = 0
        while True:
            idx = rom.find(sig, start)
            if idx == -1:
                break
            end = idx
            while end < min(len(rom), idx + 64) and 0x20 <= rom[end] <= 0xFF:
                end += 1
            preview = rom[idx:end].decode("latin-1", errors="replace")
            found.append({
                "rom_offset": f"0x{idx:06X}",
                "rom_offset_int": idx,
                "bank": idx // BANK_SIZE,
                "bank_offset": f"0x{idx % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
                "signature": sig.decode("latin-1"),
                "preview": preview,
            })
            start = idx + len(sig)
    found.sort(key=lambda x: x["rom_offset_int"])
    return found

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Locate Neto's SSF2 mapper & debug routines")
    parser.add_argument("--rom", default="Sonic_Delta_Origins_2026.bin", help="Path to 40Mbit ROM")
    parser.add_argument("--json-out", default="neto_custom_hooks.json", help="JSON report output")
    parser.add_argument("--inc-out", default="src/custom_neto/neto_hooks_map.inc", help="ASM include output")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    rom_path = Path(args.rom)
    if not rom_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"ROM not found: {rom_path}")

    rom = rom_path.read_bytes()
    ssf2_hits = scan_ssf2_accesses(rom)
    string_hits = scan_neto_strings(rom)

    report = {
        "rom": str(rom_path),
        "ssf2_access_count": len(ssf2_hits),
        "neto_string_count": len(string_hits),
        "ssf2_accesses": ssf2_hits,
        "neto_strings": string_hits,
    }

    Path(args.json_out).write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")

    inc_path = Path(args.inc_out)
    inc_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    lines = [
        "; Auto-generated Map of Neto's Custom SSF2 Mapper & Debug Hooks",
        f"; Source ROM: {rom_path.name}",
        "",
    ]
    for h in ssf2_hits:
        lines.append(
            f"Neto_SSF2_Hook_{h['rom_offset_int']:06X} equ ${h['rom_offset_int']:06X} "
            f"; Bank {h['bank']:02d} @ {h['bank_offset']} -> {h['disassembly']}"
        )
    lines.append("")
    for s in string_hits:
        safe_preview = s["preview"].replace("\\n", " ").replace("\\r", "")
        lines.append(
            f"Neto_Str_{s['rom_offset_int']:06X}       equ ${s['rom_offset_int']:06X} "
            f"; Bank {s['bank']:02d} @ {s['bank_offset']} -> \\"{safe_preview}\\""
        )

    inc_path.write_text("\\n".join(lines) + "\\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[+] Found {len(ssf2_hits)} SSF2 mapper accesses and {len(string_hits)} Neto string markers.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
'''

FILES["match_upstream.py"] = '''#!/usr/bin/env python3
import re
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

BANK_SIZE = 0x80000
MIN_ASSET_SIZE = 64
MIN_CODE_SIZE = 24
ASSET_EXTENSIONS = {".bin", ".nem", ".kos", ".kosm", ".eni", ".unc", ".wav"}

def mask_m68k_relocations(code_bytes):
    buf = bytearray(code_bytes)
    n = len(buf)
    i = 0
    while i + 1 < n:
        op = (buf[i] << 8) | buf[i + 1]
        if op in (0x4EB9, 0x4EF9) and i + 6 <= n:
            buf[i + 2 : i + 6] = b"\\x00\\x00\\x00\\x00"
            i += 6
            continue
        if (op & 0xF1FF) == 0x41F9 and i + 6 <= n:
            buf[i + 2 : i + 6] = b"\\x00\\x00\\x00\\x00"
            i += 6
            continue
        if (op & 0xF1FF) == 0x207C and i + 6 <= n:
            buf[i + 2 : i + 6] = b"\\x00\\x00\\x00\\x00"
            i += 6
            continue
        i += 2
    return bytes(buf)

def parse_as_listing(lst_path):
    symbols = []
    label_re = re.compile(r"^\\s*\\d+/\\s*([0-9A-Fa-f]+)\\s*:\\s*([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\\s*:")
    text = lst_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
    for line in text.splitlines():
        m = label_re.match(line)
        if m:
            addr = int(m.group(1), 16)
            name = m.group(2)
            if not name.startswith(("loc_", ".", "@", "+")):
                symbols.append((name, addr))

    symbols = sorted(set(symbols), key=lambda x: x[1])
    routines = []
    for idx, (name, start) in enumerate(symbols):
        end = symbols[idx + 1][1] if idx + 1 < len(symbols) else start
        size = end - start
        if MIN_CODE_SIZE <= size <= 0x4000:
Use code with caution.
routines.append((name, start, size))
return routines
def index_reference_repos(repo_dirs):
asset_prefix_index = {}
code_exact_index = {}
code_masked_index = {}
for label, root_path in repo_dirs.items():
root = Path(root_path)
if not root.exists():
print(f"[!] Warning: Reference directory not found: {root}")
continue
asset_count = 0
for filepath in root.rglob("*"):
if not filepath.is_file() or filepath.suffix.lower() not in ASSET_EXTENSIONS:
continue
data = filepath.read_bytes()
if len(data) < MIN_ASSET_SIZE:
continue
rel_path = str(filepath.relative_to(root))
entry = {"type": "asset", "repo": label, "name": rel_path, "size": len(data)}
asset_prefix_index.setdefault(data[:32], []).append((data, entry))
asset_count += 1
print(f"[+] Indexed {asset_count} binary assets from {label}")
code_count = 0
for lst_file in root.glob("*.lst"):
bin_candidate = lst_file.with_suffix(".bin")
if not bin_candidate.exists():
continue
ref_rom = bin_candidate.read_bytes()
routines = parse_as_listing(lst_file)
for sym_name, start, size in routines:
if start + size > len(ref_rom):
continue
chunk = ref_rom[start : start + size]
masked = mask_m68k_relocations(chunk)
meta = {"type": "code", "repo": label, "name": sym_name, "ref_offset": f"0x{start:06X}", "size": size}
code_exact_index.setdefault(chunk[:16], []).append((chunk, meta))
code_masked_index.setdefault(masked[:16], []).append((masked, meta))
code_count += 1
if code_count > 0:
print(f"[+] Indexed {code_count} compiled 68K routines from {label}")
return asset_prefix_index, code_exact_index, code_masked_index
def scan_rom(rom_path, asset_idx, code_exact_idx, code_masked_idx):
rom_data = Path(rom_path).read_bytes()
rom_size = len(rom_data)
print(f"[*] Scanning {rom_path} ({rom_size:,} bytes / {rom_size // BANK_SIZE} banks)...")
matches = []
pos = 0
while pos <= rom_size - MIN_CODE_SIZE:
matched = False
if pos <= rom_size - MIN_ASSET_SIZE:
p32 = rom_data[pos : pos + 32]
if p32 in asset_idx:
for ref_bytes, meta in asset_idx[p32]:
end_pos = pos + meta["size"]
if end_pos <= rom_size and rom_data[pos:end_pos] == ref_bytes:
matches.append({
"rom_offset": f"0x{pos:06X}",
"rom_offset_int": pos,
"bank": pos // BANK_SIZE,
"bank_offset": f"0x{pos % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
"size": meta["size"],
"kind": "asset",
"match_mode": "exact",
"repo": meta["repo"],
"symbol": meta["name"],
})
pos = (end_pos + 1) & ~1
matched = True
break
if matched:
continue
p16 = rom_data[pos : pos + 16]
if p16 in code_exact_idx:
for ref_bytes, meta in code_exact_idx[p16]:
end_pos = pos + meta["size"]
if end_pos <= rom_size and rom_data[pos:end_pos] == ref_bytes:
matches.append({
"rom_offset": f"0x{pos:06X}",
"rom_offset_int": pos,
"bank": pos // BANK_SIZE,
"bank_offset": f"0x{pos % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
"size": meta["size"],
"kind": "code",
"match_mode": "exact",
"repo": meta["repo"],
"symbol": meta["name"],
})
pos = (end_pos + 1) & ~1
matched = True
break
if matched:
continue
if code_masked_idx:
window_masked = mask_m68k_relocations(rom_data[pos : pos + 16])
if window_masked in code_masked_idx:
for ref_masked, meta in code_masked_idx[window_masked]:
end_pos = pos + meta["size"]
if end_pos <= rom_size:
cand_masked = mask_m68k_relocations(rom_data[pos:end_pos])
if cand_masked == ref_masked:
matches.append({
"rom_offset": f"0x{pos:06X}",
"rom_offset_int": pos,
"bank": pos // BANK_SIZE,
"bank_offset": f"0x{pos % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
"size": meta["size"],
"kind": "code",
"match_mode": "relocated",
"repo": meta["repo"],
"symbol": meta["name"],
})
pos = (end_pos + 1) & ~1
matched = True
break
if matched:
continue
pos += 2
return matches
def main():
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Match upstream assets & 68K code against 40Mb ROM")
parser.add_argument("--rom", default="Sonic_Delta_Origins_2026.bin", help="Path to 40Mbit ROM")
parser.add_argument("--s1", default="s1disasm-AS", help="Path to s1disasm-AS")
parser.add_argument("--s2", default="s2disasm-master", help="Path to s2disasm-master")
parser.add_argument("--sk", default="skdisasm-master", help="Path to skdisasm-master")
parser.add_argument("--out", default="upstream_matches.json", help="Output JSON report")
args = parser.parse_args()
repos = {"s1disasm": args.s1, "s2disasm": args.s2, "skdisasm": args.sk}
asset_idx, code_exact_idx, code_masked_idx = index_reference_repos(repos)
matches = scan_rom(args.rom, asset_idx, code_exact_idx, code_masked_idx)
bank_summary = {}
for m in matches:
b = m["bank"]
bank_summary[b] = bank_summary.get(b, 0) + m["size"]
report = {
"rom": args.rom,
"total_matches": len(matches),
"asset_matches": sum(1 for m in matches if m["kind"] == "asset"),
"code_matches": sum(1 for m in matches if m["kind"] == "code"),
"matched_bytes_per_bank": {f"bank_{k:02d}": v for k, v in sorted(bank_summary.items())},
"matches": matches,
}
Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(f"[+] Saved {len(matches)} total matches ({report['asset_matches']} assets, {report['code_matches']} code) -> {args.out}")
if name == "main":
main()
'''

FILES["trace_ssf2_live.lua"] = '''-- trace_ssf2_live.lua
-- Unified MAME & Mesen Lua script for logging Sega SSF2 mapper & SRAM writes ($A130F1 - $A130FF)
local LOG_FILE = "trace_banks.txt"
local out = assert(io.open(LOG_FILE, "w"))
out:write("# Sonic Delta 40Mb Live SSF2 Mapper Trace\n")
out:write("# Format: PC_HEX | REGISTER | VALUE_HEX | SLOT_RANGE\n")
out:flush()
local REG_NAMES = {
[0xA130F1] = { "SSF2_SRAM_CTRL", "SRAM/ROM Toggle ($200000-$3FFFFF)" },
[0xA130F3] = { "SSF2_BANK_1", "Slot 1 ($080000-$0FFFFF)" },
[0xA130F5] = { "SSF2_BANK_2", "Slot 2 ($100000-$17FFFF)" },
[0xA130F7] = { "SSF2_BANK_3", "Slot 3 ($180000-$1FFFFF)" },
[0xA130F9] = { "SSF2_BANK_4", "Slot 4 ($200000-$27FFFF)" },
[0xA130FB] = { "SSF2_BANK_5", "Slot 5 ($280000-$2FFFFF)" },
[0xA130FD] = { "SSF2_BANK_6", "Slot 6 ($300000-$37FFFF)" },
[0xA130FF] = { "SSF2_BANK_7", "Slot 7 ($380000-$3FFFFF)" },
}
local function log_write(pc, addr, val)
local info = REG_NAMES[addr]
if not info then return end
local line = string.format("0x%06X | %-14s | 0x%02X | %s\n", pc & 0xFFFFFF, info[1], val & 0xFF, info[2])
out:write(line)
out:flush()
print("[SSF2] " .. line:gsub("\n", ""))
end
if emu and emu.addMemoryCallback then
emu.addMemoryCallback(function(addr, val)
local state = emu.getState()
local pc = state["cpu.pc"] or state["pc"] or 0
log_write(pc, addr, val)
end, emu.callbackType.write, 0xA130F1, 0xA130FF)
elseif manager and manager.machine then
local cpu = manager.machine.devices[":maincpu"]
local space = cpu.spaces["program"]
space:install_write_tap(0xA130F1, 0xA130FF, "ssf2_mapper_tap", function(offset, data, mask)
local pc = cpu.state["PC"].value
log_write(pc, offset, data)
return data
end)
end

FILES["collect_trace.py"] = '''#!/usr/bin/env python3
import re
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
BANK_SIZE = 0x80000
SLOT_WINDOWS = {
"SSF2_SRAM_CTRL": ("SRAM/ROM Control", 0x200000),
"SSF2_BANK_1": ("Slot 1", 0x080000),
"SSF2_BANK_2": ("Slot 2", 0x100000),
"SSF2_BANK_3": ("Slot 3", 0x180000),
"SSF2_BANK_4": ("Slot 4", 0x200000),
"SSF2_BANK_5": ("Slot 5", 0x280000),
"SSF2_BANK_6": ("Slot 6", 0x300000),
"SSF2_BANK_7": ("Slot 7", 0x380000),
}
TRACE_LINE_RE = re.compile(
r"^\s0x([0-9A-Fa-f]+)\s\|\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)\s*\|\s0x([0-9A-Fa-f]+)\s(?:\|\s*(.*))?$"
)
def parse_trace_file(trace_path: Path):
if not trace_path.exists():
raise FileNotFoundError(f"Trace log not found: {trace_path}")
events = []
seen_pairs = {}
for raw_line in trace_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines():
line = raw_line.strip()
if not line or line.startswith("#") or line.startswith("Hardware watchpoint"):
continue
m = TRACE_LINE_RE.match(line)
if not m:
continue
pc_val = int(m.group(1), 16) & 0xFFFFFF
reg_name = m.group(2).strip()
bank_val = int(m.group(3), 16) & 0xFF
note = (m.group(4) or "").strip()
key = (pc_val, reg_name, bank_val)
seen_pairs[key] = seen_pairs.get(key, 0) + 1
if seen_pairs[key] == 1:
slot_label, base_vaddr = SLOT_WINDOWS.get(reg_name, ("Unknown Slot", 0))
target_rom_offset = f"0x{bank_val * BANK_SIZE:06X}" if reg_name != "SSF2_SRAM_CTRL" else None
events.append({
"pc": f"0x{pc_val:06X}",
"pc_int": pc_val,
"pc_bank": pc_val // BANK_SIZE,
"pc_bank_offset": f"0x{pc_val % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
"register": reg_name,
"slot": slot_label,
"mapped_window_start": f"0x{base_vaddr:06X}",
"value_written": f"0x{bank_val:02X}",
"value_int": bank_val,
"target_rom_offset": target_rom_offset,
"note": note,
"hit_count": 1,
})
for ev in events:
key = (ev["pc_int"], ev["register"], ev["value_int"])
ev["hit_count"] = seen_pairs[key]
return events
def main():
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Parse MAME/Mesen/BlastEm SSF2 trace logs")
parser.add_argument("--trace", default="trace_banks.txt", help="Input trace log path")
parser.add_argument("--out", default="resolved_switches.json", help="Output JSON path")
args = parser.parse_args()
events = parse_trace_file(Path(args.trace))
slot_summary = {}
for ev in events:
slot_summary.setdefault(ev["register"], set()).add(ev["value_written"])
report = {
"source_trace": args.trace,
"unique_switch_sites": len(events),
"total_logged_writes": sum(e["hit_count"] for e in events),
"observed_values_per_register": {k: sorted(v) for k, v in sorted(slot_summary.items())},
"switches": events,
}
Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(f"[+] Parsed {report['total_logged_writes']} SSF2 writes across {len(events)} unique PCs -> {args.out}")
if name == "main":
main()
'''
FILES["ghidra_auto_bank.py"] = '''# ghidra_auto_bank.py (v3 + Runtime Switch Importer)
import os
import json
from ghidra.program.model.symbol import SourceType
from ghidra.program.model.data import ByteDataType, ArrayDataType
from ghidra.app.cmd.disassemble import DisassembleCommand
def sanitize_symbol(name):
clean = "".join(c if c.isalnum() else "" for c in name)
while "" in clean:
clean = clean.replace("", "")
return clean.strip("_")
def label_genesis_hardware():
hw_regs = {
0x00A10001: "HW_VERSION",
0x00A10003: "JOYPAD_1_DATA",
0x00A10005: "JOYPAD_2_DATA",
0x00A10009: "JOYPAD_1_CTRL",
0x00A1000B: "JOYPAD_2_CTRL",
0x00A11100: "Z80_BUSREQ",
0x00A11200: "Z80_RESET",
0x00A130F1: "SSF2_SRAM_CTRL",
0x00A130F3: "SSF2_BANK_1",
0x00A130F5: "SSF2_BANK_2",
0x00A130F7: "SSF2_BANK_3",
0x00A130F9: "SSF2_BANK_4",
0x00A130FB: "SSF2_BANK_5",
0x00A130FD: "SSF2_BANK_6",
0x00A130FF: "SSF2_BANK_7",
0x00C00000: "VDP_DATA",
0x00C00004: "VDP_CTRL",
0x00C00008: "VDP_HV_COUNTER",
0x00C00011: "PSG_CTRL",
}
sym_table = currentProgram.getSymbolTable()
space = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
for addr_int, name in hw_regs.items():
try:
sym_table.createLabel(space.getAddress(addr_int), name, SourceType.USER_DEFINED)
except Exception:
pass
def apply_ram_symbols():
ram_path = "upstream_ram_symbols.json"
if not os.path.exists(ram_path):
return
with open(ram_path, "r") as f:
ram_map = json.load(f)
sym_table = currentProgram.getSymbolTable()
space = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
for _, info in ram_map.items():
addr = space.getAddress(info["address_int"])
names = info.get("names", [])
if names:
primary_name = sanitize_symbol("or".join(names[:3]))
try:
sym_table.createLabel(addr, primary_name, SourceType.USER_DEFINED)
except Exception:
pass
def apply_upstream_matches(bank_index):
matches_path = "upstream_matches.json"
if not os.path.exists(matches_path):
return
with open(matches_path, "r") as f:
report = json.load(f)
sym_table = currentProgram.getSymbolTable()
listing = currentProgram.getListing()
space = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
base_addr = currentProgram.getImageBase().getOffset()
for m in report.get("matches", []):
if m.get("bank") != bank_index:
continue
local_offset = int(m["bank_offset"], 16)
size = m["size"]
kind = m["kind"]
repo = m["repo"]
sym_raw = m["symbol"]
mode = m.get("match_mode", "exact")
target_addr = space.getAddress(base_addr + local_offset)
sym_name = "{}{}{:05X}".format(repo, sanitize_symbol(sym_raw), local_offset)
try:
sym_table.createLabel(target_addr, sym_name, SourceType.USER_DEFINED)
listing.setComment(target_addr, 0, "[UPSTREAM: {} | {} | {} | {} bytes]".format(repo, kind, mode, size))
except Exception:
pass
if kind == "asset":
end_addr = space.getAddress(base_addr + local_offset + size - 1)
listing.clearCodeUnits(target_addr, end_addr, False)
try:
listing.createData(target_addr, ArrayDataType(ByteDataType.dataType, size, 1))
except Exception:
pass
elif kind == "code":
DisassembleCommand(target_addr, None, True).applyTo(currentProgram)
def apply_neto_hooks(bank_index):
hooks_path = "neto_custom_hooks.json"
if not os.path.exists(hooks_path):
return
with open(hooks_path, "r") as f:
report = json.load(f)
sym_table = currentProgram.getSymbolTable()
listing = currentProgram.getListing()
space = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
base_addr = currentProgram.getImageBase().getOffset()
for h in report.get("ssf2_accesses", []):
if h.get("bank") != bank_index:
continue
local_offset = int(h["bank_offset"], 16)
target_addr = space.getAddress(base_addr + local_offset)
lbl = "Neto_SSF2_{}_{:05X}".format(h["register_name"], local_offset)
try:
sym_table.createLabel(target_addr, lbl, SourceType.USER_DEFINED)
listing.setComment(target_addr, 0, "[NETO CUSTOM: {}]".format(h["disassembly"]))
DisassembleCommand(target_addr, None, True).applyTo(currentProgram)
except Exception:
pass
def apply_runtime_switches(bank_index):
switches_path = "resolved_switches.json"
if not os.path.exists(switches_path):
return
with open(switches_path, "r") as f:
report = json.load(f)
sym_table = currentProgram.getSymbolTable()
listing = currentProgram.getListing()
space = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
base_addr = currentProgram.getImageBase().getOffset()
by_pc = {}
for sw in report.get("switches", []):
if sw.get("pc_bank") == bank_index:
by_pc.setdefault(int(sw["pc_bank_offset"], 16), []).append(sw)
for local_off, sw_list in sorted(by_pc.items()):
target_addr = space.getAddress(base_addr + local_off)
regs = sorted(set(s["register"] for s in sw_list))
vals = sorted(set(s["value_written"] for s in sw_list))
lbl = "Runtime_SSF2_{}{:05X}".format("".join(regs), local_off)
comment = "[RUNTIME TRACE: {} <- {} ({} hits)]".format(",".join(regs), ",".join(vals), sum(s["hit_count"] for s in sw_list))
try:
sym_table.createLabel(target_addr, lbl, SourceType.USER_DEFINED)
listing.setComment(target_addr, 0, comment)
except Exception:
pass
def run():
prog_name = currentProgram.getName()
bank_index = 0
for part in prog_name.replace(".", "").split(""):
if part.isdigit():
bank_index = int(part)
break
label_genesis_hardware()
apply_ram_symbols()
apply_upstream_matches(bank_index)
apply_neto_hooks(bank_index)
apply_runtime_switches(bank_index)
run()
'''
FILES["split_to_repo.py"] = '''#!/usr/bin/env python3
import re
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
BANK_SIZE = 0x80000
LABEL_LINE_RE = re.compile(r"^([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_])\s:")
def sanitize_label(path_str):
stem = Path(path_str).stem
clean = "".join(c if c.isalnum() else "" for c in stem)
while "" in clean:
clean = clean.replace("", "")
return clean.strip("_")
def classify_symbol(label_name):
for prefix in ("s1disasm", "s2disasm", "skdisasm"):
if label_name.startswith(prefix + ""):
return prefix
if label_name.startswith("Neto"):
return "custom_neto"
return None
def slice_bank_asm_by_symbols(bank_file, out_root, master_includes):
bank_stem = bank_file.stem
lines = bank_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines()
segments = []
cur_folder = "banks"
cur_name = f"{bank_stem}_header"
cur_lines = []
for line in lines:
m = LABEL_LINE_RE.match(line.strip())
if m:
lbl = m.group(1)
folder = classify_symbol(lbl)
if folder:
if cur_lines:
segments.append((cur_folder, cur_name, cur_lines))
cur_folder = folder
cur_name = lbl
cur_lines = [line]
continue
cur_lines.append(line)
if cur_lines:
segments.append((cur_folder, cur_name, cur_lines))
for idx, (folder, mod_name, seg_lines) in enumerate(segments):
if folder == "banks":
rel_path = Path("src") / "banks" / f"{bank_stem}_part{idx:03d}.asm"
else:
rel_path = Path("src") / folder / f"{mod_name}.asm"
dest = out_root / rel_path
dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
dest.write_text("\n".join(seg_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
master_includes.append(f' include "{rel_path.as_posix()}"')
def build_repo_structure(rom_path, matches_json, hooks_json, asm_dir, out_repo):
rom_data = Path(rom_path).read_bytes()
report = json.loads(Path(matches_json).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
matches = report.get("matches", [])
out = Path(out_repo)
for sub in ("src/banks", "src/custom_neto", "src/s1disasm", "src/s2disasm", "src/skdisasm", "assets"):
(out / sub).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
asset_inc_lines = ["; Auto-generated Upstream Asset Table", ""]
asset_count = 0
for m in matches:
if m.get("kind") != "asset":
continue
start = m["rom_offset_int"]
size = m["size"]
repo_tag = m["repo"]
rel_upstream = Path(m["symbol"])
dest_file = out / "assets" / repo_tag / rel_upstream
dest_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
dest_file.write_bytes(rom_data[start : start + size])
lbl = f"{repo_tag}{sanitize_label(str(rel_upstream))}{start:06X}"
rel_inc = dest_file.relative_to(out).as_posix()
asset_inc_lines.append(f"{lbl}:")
asset_inc_lines.append(f' incbin "{rel_inc}"')
asset_inc_lines.append("")
asset_count += 1
(out / "src" / "upstream_assets.asm").write_text("\n".join(asset_inc_lines), encoding="utf-8")
mapper_inc = """SRAM_CTRL equ $A130F1
BANK_REG_1 equ $A130F3
BANK_REG_2 equ $A130F5
BANK_REG_3 equ $A130F7
BANK_REG_4 equ $A130F9
BANK_REG_5 equ $A130FB
BANK_REG_6 equ $A130FD
BANK_REG_7 equ $A130FF
SET_BANK macro reg, bank_id
move.b #\\bank_id, (\\reg).l
endm
"""
(out / "src" / "ssf2_mapper.inc").write_text(mapper_inc, encoding="utf-8")
neto_inc_local = Path("src/custom_neto/neto_hooks_map.inc")
has_neto_inc = False
if neto_inc_local.exists():
(out / "src" / "custom_neto" / "neto_hooks_map.inc").write_text(
neto_inc_local.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), encoding="utf-8"
)
has_neto_inc = True
master_lines = [
' include "src/ssf2_mapper.inc"',
' include "src/upstream_assets.asm"',
]
if has_neto_inc:
master_lines.append(' include "src/custom_neto/neto_hooks_map.inc"')
master_lines.append("")
asm_source = Path(asm_dir)
bank_files = sorted(asm_source.glob("bank_*.asm")) if asm_source.exists() else []
for bf in bank_files:
slice_bank_asm_by_symbols(bf, out, master_lines)
(out / "main.asm").write_text("\n".join(master_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
makefile_text = f"""VASM = vasmm68k_mot
VASMFLAGS = -Fbin -spaces -devpac
TARGET = build/sonic_delta_40mb_rebuilt.bin
ORIG_ROM = {rom_path}
all: $(TARGET) verify
$(TARGET): main.asm
@mkdir -p build
$(VASM) $(VASMFLAGS) -o $(TARGET) main.asm
verify: $(TARGET)
@cmp -l $(ORIG_ROM) $(TARGET) | head -n 20 || true
@sha256sum $(ORIG_ROM) $(TARGET)
clean:
rm -rf build
"""
(out / "Makefile").write_text(makefile_text, encoding="utf-8")
print(f"[+] Generated repository at {out.resolve()} ({asset_count} assets, {len(bank_files)} banks)")
def main():
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Build modular Git repo from 40Mb ROM & matches")
parser.add_argument("--rom", default="Sonic_Delta_Origins_2026.bin")
parser.add_argument("--matches", default="upstream_matches.json")
parser.add_argument("--hooks", default="neto_custom_hooks.json")
parser.add_argument("--asm-dir", default="disasm_banks")
parser.add_argument("--out", default="sonic_delta_disasm_repo")
args = parser.parse_args()
build_repo_structure(args.rom, args.matches, args.hooks, args.asm_dir, args.out)
if name == "main":
main()
'''
FILES["xref_enricher.py"] = '''#!/usr/bin/env python3
import re
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
UPSTREAM_ROOTS = {
"s1disasm": Path("s1disasm-AS"),
"s2disasm": Path("s2disasm-master"),
"skdisasm": Path("skdisasm-master"),
}
HEX_OPERAND_RE = re.compile(r"\($([0-9A-Fa-f]{4,8})\)\.(w|l)")
NETO_HOOK_LABEL_RE = re.compile(r"^Neto_SSF2_[A-Z0-9_]+_([0-9A-Fa-f]{6})\s*:")
def sanitize_symbol(name):
clean = "".join(c if c.isalnum() else "" for c in name)
while "" in clean:
clean = clean.replace("", "")
return clean.strip("_")
def locate_upstream_source(repo_tag, symbol_name):
root = UPSTREAM_ROOTS.get(repo_tag)
if not root or not root.exists():
return None
raw_sym = symbol_name
for prefix in ("s1disasm_", "s2disasm_", "skdisasm_"):
if raw_sym.startswith(prefix):
raw_sym = raw_sym[len(prefix):]
raw_sym = re.sub(r"_[0-9A-Fa-f]{5,6}$", "", raw_sym)
pattern = re.compile(rf"^\s*{re.escape(raw_sym)}\s*:", re.MULTILINE)
for asm_file in root.rglob("*.asm"):
try:
text = asm_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
except Exception:
continue
m = pattern.search(text)
if m:
line_no = text[: m.start()].count("\n") + 1
return f"{asm_file.as_posix()}:{line_no} ({raw_sym})"
return None
def load_runtime_switches(switches_path):
by_pc = {}
p = Path(switches_path)
if not p.exists():
return by_pc
data = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
for sw in data.get("switches", []):
by_pc.setdefault(sw["pc_int"], []).append(sw)
return by_pc
def format_runtime_comment(sw_list):
parts = []
for sw in sw_list:
val = sw["value_written"]
hits = sw["hit_count"]
target = sw.get("target_rom_offset")
if target:
parts.append(f"{sw['register']}<-{val} (ROM {target}, {hits}x)")
else:
parts.append(f"{sw['register']}<-{val} ({hits}x)")
return "; [RUNTIME TRACE: " + " | ".join(parts) + "]"
def build_symbol_tables(matches_path, ram_path, hooks_path, switches_by_pc):
addr_to_label = {}
const_defs = {}
hw = {
0x00A10001: "HW_VERSION",
0x00A10003: "JOYPAD_1_DATA",
0x00A10005: "JOYPAD_2_DATA",
0x00A10009: "JOYPAD_1_CTRL",
0x00A1000B: "JOYPAD_2_CTRL",
0x00A11100: "Z80_BUSREQ",
0x00A11200: "Z80_RESET",
0x00C00000: "VDP_DATA",
0x00C00004: "VDP_CTRL",
0x00C00008: "VDP_HV_COUNTER",
0x00C00011: "PSG_CTRL",
}
for addr, name in hw.items():
addr_to_label[addr] = name
const_defs[name] = f"${addr:08X}"
if Path(ram_path).exists():
ram_data = json.loads(Path(ram_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
for _, info in ram_data.items():
addr = info["address_int"]
names = info.get("names", [])
if names:
lbl = sanitize_symbol(names[0])
addr_to_label[addr] = lbl
const_defs[lbl] = f"${addr:08X}"
if Path(matches_path).exists():
match_data = json.loads(Path(matches_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
for m in match_data.get("matches", []):
addr = m["rom_offset_int"]
lbl = f"{m['repo']}{sanitize_symbol(m['symbol'])}{addr:06X}"
addr_to_label[addr] = lbl
const_defs[lbl] = f"${addr:08X}"
if Path(hooks_path).exists():
hook_data = json.loads(Path(hooks_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
for h in hook_data.get("ssf2_accesses", []):
addr = h["rom_offset_int"]
lbl = f"Neto_SSF2_{h['register_name']}_{addr:06X}"
addr_to_label[addr] = lbl
const_defs[lbl] = f"${addr:08X}"
for pc_int, sw_list in switches_by_pc.items():
if pc_int not in addr_to_label:
regs = "".join(sorted(set(s["register"] for s in sw_list)))
lbl = f"Runtime_SSF2{regs}_{pc_int:06X}"
addr_to_label[pc_int] = lbl
const_defs[lbl] = f"${pc_int:08X}"
return addr_to_label, const_defs
def write_runtime_switches_inc(src_dir, switches_by_pc):
if not switches_by_pc:
return False
custom_dir = src_dir / "custom_neto"
custom_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
out_inc = custom_dir / "runtime_bank_switches.inc"
lines = ["; Live Runtime SSF2 Bank Switch Summary", ""]
for pc_int in sorted(switches_by_pc.keys()):
sw_list = switches_by_pc[pc_int]
regs = "".join(sorted(set(s["register"] for s in sw_list)))
lbl = f"Runtime_SSF2{regs}_{pc_int:06X}"
lines.append(f"{lbl:<44} equ ${pc_int:06X} {format_runtime_comment(sw_list)}")
out_inc.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
return True
def enrich_repository(repo_dir, addr_to_label, const_defs, switches_by_pc):
repo = Path(repo_dir)
src_dir = repo / "src"
inc_lines = ["; Auto-generated Cross-Referenced Human-Readable Symbols", ""]
for name, hex_val in sorted(const_defs.items()):
inc_lines.append(f"{name:<44} equ {hex_val}")
(src_dir / "human_constants.inc").write_text("\n".join(inc_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
has_runtime_inc = write_runtime_switches_inc(src_dir, switches_by_pc)
main_asm = repo / "main.asm"
if main_asm.exists():
main_txt = main_asm.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
headers = [' include "src/human_constants.inc"']
if has_runtime_inc:
headers.append(' include "src/custom_neto/runtime_bank_switches.inc"')
missing = [h for h in headers if h not in main_txt]
if missing:
main_asm.write_text("\n".join(missing) + "\n" + main_txt, encoding="utf-8")
for asm_file in src_dir.rglob("*.asm"):
if asm_file.name == "upstream_assets.asm":
continue
lines = asm_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines()
new_lines = []
changed = False
stem = asm_file.stem
for repo_tag in ("s1disasm", "s2disasm", "skdisasm"):
if stem.startswith(repo_tag + "_"):
xref_loc = locate_upstream_source(repo_tag, stem)
if xref_loc and (not lines or "XREF Upstream Source:" not in lines[0]):
new_lines.append(f"; XREF Upstream Source: {xref_loc}")
changed = True
break
for line in lines:
m_hook = NETO_HOOK_LABEL_RE.match(line.strip())
if m_hook and switches_by_pc:
hook_pc = int(m_hook.group(1), 16)
if hook_pc in switches_by_pc:
rt_comment = format_runtime_comment(switches_by_pc[hook_pc])
if not new_lines or new_lines[-1] != rt_comment:
new_lines.append(rt_comment)
changed = True
def _sub_addr(match):
nonlocal changed
raw_hex = match.group(1)
size_suffix = match.group(2)
val = int(raw_hex, 16)
if size_suffix == "w" and val >= 0x8000:
val = 0x00FF0000 | val
lbl = addr_to_label.get(val)
if lbl:
changed = True
return f"({lbl}).{size_suffix}"
return match.group(0)
new_lines.append(HEX_OPERAND_RE.sub(_sub_addr, line))
if changed:
asm_file.write_text("\n".join(new_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
def main():
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Enrich split disassembly with cross-references")
parser.add_argument("--repo", default="sonic_delta_disasm_repo")
parser.add_argument("--matches", default="upstream_matches.json")
parser.add_argument("--ram", default="upstream_ram_symbols.json")
parser.add_argument("--hooks", default="neto_custom_hooks.json")
parser.add_argument("--switches", default="resolved_switches.json")
args = parser.parse_args()
switches_by_pc = load_runtime_switches(args.switches)
addr_to_label, const_defs = build_symbol_tables(args.matches, args.ram, args.hooks, switches_by_pc)
enrich_repository(args.repo, addr_to_label, const_defs, switches_by_pc)
if name == "main":
main()
'''

File 1: import_upstream_constants.py
Extracts Work RAM, Z80 RAM, and VDP/I/O symbols from s1disasm-AS, s2disasm-master, and skdisasm-master into upstream_ram_symbols.json.
python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import re
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

CONSTANT_FILES = [
    ("s1", "s1disasm-AS/_Constants.asm"),
    ("s1", "s1disasm-AS/_Variables.asm"),
    ("s2", "s2disasm-master/s2.constants.asm"),
    ("s3", "skdisasm-master/s3.constants.asm"),
    ("sk", "skdisasm-master/sk.constants.asm"),
]

DEF_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\s*([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\s*(?:=|equ|EQU)\s*\$([0-9A-Fa-f]{4,8})\b"
)

def normalize_68k_ram_addr(val):
    addr = val & 0x00FFFFFF
    if 0x8000 <= val <= 0xFFFF:
        addr = 0x00FF0000 | val
    return addr

def is_valid_hardware_or_ram(addr):
    return (
        (0x00FF0000 <= addr <= 0x00FFFFFF)
        or (0x00A00000 <= addr <= 0x00A01FFF)
        or (0x00A10000 <= addr <= 0x00C0001F)
    )

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Extract upstream RAM & hardware symbols")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default="upstream_ram_symbols.json", help="Output JSON path")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    symbols_by_addr = {}
    parsed_files = 0

    for prefix, rel_path in CONSTANT_FILES:
        p = Path(rel_path)
        if not p.exists():
            print(f"[!] Skipping missing file: {p}")
            continue

        parsed_files += 1
        text = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
        for line in text.splitlines():
            code_part = line.split(";")[0]
            m = DEF_RE.match(code_part)
            if not m:
                continue
            name = m.group(1)
            raw_val = int(m.group(2), 16)
            addr = normalize_68k_ram_addr(raw_val)
            if not is_valid_hardware_or_ram(addr):
                continue

            addr_key = f"0x{addr:06X}"
            entry = symbols_by_addr.setdefault(addr_key, {"address_int": addr, "names": []})
            tagged_name = f"{prefix}_{name}"
            if tagged_name not in entry["names"]:
                entry["names"].append(tagged_name)

    Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(symbols_by_addr, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[+] Parsed {parsed_files} constant files -> {len(symbols_by_addr)} unique addresses saved to {args.out}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
Use code with caution.

File 2: extract_neto_custom.py
Scans Sonic_Delta_Origins_2026.bin for SSF2 mapper accesses ($A130F1–$A130FF) and Neto's custom debug strings (Exceção $41, Super Depurador), generating neto_custom_hooks.json and src/custom_neto/neto_hooks_map.inc.
python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json
import struct
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

BANK_SIZE = 0x80000

SSF2_REGS = {
    0x00A130F1: "SSF2_SRAM_CTRL",
    0x00A130F3: "SSF2_BANK_1",
    0x00A130F5: "SSF2_BANK_2",
    0x00A130F7: "SSF2_BANK_3",
    0x00A130F9: "SSF2_BANK_4",
    0x00A130FB: "SSF2_BANK_5",
    0x00A130FD: "SSF2_BANK_6",
    0x00A130FF: "SSF2_BANK_7",
}

NETO_SIGNATURES = [
    b"Neto - Depurador",
    b"Super Depurador",
    b"Mapeador n",
    b"SRAM n",
    b"Sonic Delta Next",
    b"SONIC DELTA 40Mb",
    b"M68K Bank",
    b"neto.baiano",
]

def decode_simple_m68k(rom, offset):
    if offset + 2 > len(rom):
        return "dc.w $0000", 2

    op = struct.unpack(">H", rom[offset : offset + 2])[0]

    if op == 0x13FC and offset + 8 <= len(rom):
        imm = struct.unpack(">H", rom[offset + 2 : offset + 4])[0] & 0xFF
        addr = struct.unpack(">I", rom[offset + 4 : offset + 8])[0]
        reg_name = SSF2_REGS.get(addr, f"${addr:08X}")
        return f"move.b  #${imm:02X}, ({reg_name}).l", 8

    if (op & 0xFFF8) == 0x13C0 and offset + 6 <= len(rom):
        dn = op & 0x0007
        addr = struct.unpack(">I", rom[offset + 2 : offset + 6])[0]
        reg_name = SSF2_REGS.get(addr, f"${addr:08X}")
        return f"move.b  d{dn}, ({reg_name}).l", 6

    if op == 0x4A39 and offset + 6 <= len(rom):
        addr = struct.unpack(">I", rom[offset + 2 : offset + 6])[0]
        reg_name = SSF2_REGS.get(addr, f"${addr:08X}")
        return f"tst.b   ({reg_name}).l", 6

    if op == 0x4EB9 and offset + 6 <= len(rom):
        addr = struct.unpack(">I", rom[offset + 2 : offset + 6])[0]
        return f"jsr     (${addr:08X}).l", 6

    if op == 0x4EF9 and offset + 6 <= len(rom):
        addr = struct.unpack(">I", rom[offset + 2 : offset + 6])[0]
        return f"jmp     (${addr:08X}).l", 6

    if op == 0x4E75:
        return "rts", 2
    if op == 0x4E73:
        return "rte", 2

    return f"dc.w    ${op:04X}", 2

def scan_ssf2_accesses(rom):
    hits = []
    n = len(rom)
    for pos in range(0, n - 4, 2):
        val = struct.unpack(">I", rom[pos : pos + 4])[0]
        if val in SSF2_REGS:
            reg_name = SSF2_REGS[val]
            inst_offset = pos - 2
            if pos >= 4:
                prev2 = struct.unpack(">H", rom[pos - 4 : pos - 2])[0]
                if prev2 == 0x13FC:
                    inst_offset = pos - 4

            disasm, size = decode_simple_m68k(rom, inst_offset)
            hits.append({
                "rom_offset": f"0x{inst_offset:06X}",
                "rom_offset_int": inst_offset,
                "bank": inst_offset // BANK_SIZE,
                "bank_offset": f"0x{inst_offset % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
                "register": f"0x{val:06X}",
                "register_name": reg_name,
                "disassembly": disasm,
                "bytes": rom[inst_offset : inst_offset + size].hex().upper(),
            })
    return hits

def scan_neto_strings(rom):
    found = []
    for sig in NETO_SIGNATURES:
        start = 0
        while True:
            idx = rom.find(sig, start)
            if idx == -1:
                break
            end = idx
            while end < min(len(rom), idx + 64) and 0x20 <= rom[end] <= 0xFF:
                end += 1
            preview = rom[idx:end].decode("latin-1", errors="replace")
            found.append({
                "rom_offset": f"0x{idx:06X}",
                "rom_offset_int": idx,
                "bank": idx // BANK_SIZE,
                "bank_offset": f"0x{idx % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
                "signature": sig.decode("latin-1"),
                "preview": preview,
            })
            start = idx + len(sig)
    found.sort(key=lambda x: x["rom_offset_int"])
    return found

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Locate Neto's SSF2 mapper & debug routines")
    parser.add_argument("--rom", default="Sonic_Delta_Origins_2026.bin", help="Path to 40Mbit ROM")
    parser.add_argument("--json-out", default="neto_custom_hooks.json", help="JSON report output")
    parser.add_argument("--inc-out", default="src/custom_neto/neto_hooks_map.inc", help="ASM include output")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    rom_path = Path(args.rom)
    if not rom_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"ROM not found: {rom_path}")

    rom = rom_path.read_bytes()
    ssf2_hits = scan_ssf2_accesses(rom)
    string_hits = scan_neto_strings(rom)

    report = {
        "rom": str(rom_path),
        "ssf2_access_count": len(ssf2_hits),
        "neto_string_count": len(string_hits),
        "ssf2_accesses": ssf2_hits,
        "neto_strings": string_hits,
    }

    Path(args.json_out).write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")

    inc_path = Path(args.inc_out)
    inc_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    lines = [
        "; Auto-generated Map of Neto's Custom SSF2 Mapper & Debug Hooks",
        f"; Source ROM: {rom_path.name}",
        "",
    ]
    for h in ssf2_hits:
        lines.append(
            f"Neto_SSF2_Hook_{h['rom_offset_int']:06X} equ ${h['rom_offset_int']:06X} "
            f"; Bank {h['bank']:02d} @ {h['bank_offset']} -> {h['disassembly']}"
        )
    lines.append("")
    for s in string_hits:
        safe_preview = s["preview"].replace("\n", " ").replace("\r", "")
        lines.append(
            f"Neto_Str_{s['rom_offset_int']:06X}       equ ${s['rom_offset_int']:06X} "
            f"; Bank {s['bank']:02d} @ {s['bank_offset']} -> \"{safe_preview}\""
        )

    inc_path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[+] Found {len(ssf2_hits)} SSF2 mapper accesses and {len(string_hits)} Neto string markers.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
Use code with caution.

yes
Here is File 3 (match_upstream.py v2), which scans all 10 banks of Sonic_Delta_Origins_2026.bin for verbatim upstream binary assets and exact/relocated 68000 code routines.
File 3: match_upstream.py
python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import re
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

BANK_SIZE = 0x80000
MIN_ASSET_SIZE = 64
MIN_CODE_SIZE = 24
ASSET_EXTENSIONS = {".bin", ".nem", ".kos", ".kosm", ".eni", ".unc", ".wav"}

def mask_m68k_relocations(code_bytes):
    buf = bytearray(code_bytes)
    n = len(buf)
    i = 0
    while i + 1 < n:
        op = (buf[i] << 8) | buf[i + 1]
        if op in (0x4EB9, 0x4EF9) and i + 6 <= n:
            buf[i + 2 : i + 6] = b"\x00\x00\x00\x00"
            i += 6
            continue
        if (op & 0xF1FF) == 0x41F9 and i + 6 <= n:
            buf[i + 2 : i + 6] = b"\x00\x00\x00\x00"
            i += 6
            continue
        if (op & 0xF1FF) == 0x207C and i + 6 <= n:
            buf[i + 2 : i + 6] = b"\x00\x00\x00\x00"
            i += 6
            continue
        i += 2
    return bytes(buf)

def parse_as_listing(lst_path):
    symbols = []
    label_re = re.compile(r"^\s*\d+/\s*([0-9A-Fa-f]+)\s*:\s*([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\s*:")
    text = lst_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
    for line in text.splitlines():
        m = label_re.match(line)
        if m:
            addr = int(m.group(1), 16)
            name = m.group(2)
            if not name.startswith(("loc_", ".", "@", "+")):
                symbols.append((name, addr))

    symbols = sorted(set(symbols), key=lambda x: x[1])
    routines = []
    for idx, (name, start) in enumerate(symbols):
        end = symbols[idx + 1][1] if idx + 1 < len(symbols) else start
        size = end - start
        if MIN_CODE_SIZE <= size <= 0x4000:
            routines.append((name, start, size))
    return routines

def index_reference_repos(repo_dirs):
    asset_prefix_index = {}
    code_exact_index = {}
    code_masked_index = {}

    for label, root_path in repo_dirs.items():
        root = Path(root_path)
        if not root.exists():
            print(f"[!] Warning: Reference directory not found: {root}")
            continue

        asset_count = 0
        for filepath in root.rglob("*"):
            if not filepath.is_file() or filepath.suffix.lower() not in ASSET_EXTENSIONS:
                continue
            data = filepath.read_bytes()
            if len(data) < MIN_ASSET_SIZE:
                continue

            rel_path = str(filepath.relative_to(root))
            entry = {"type": "asset", "repo": label, "name": rel_path, "size": len(data)}
            asset_prefix_index.setdefault(data[:32], []).append((data, entry))
            asset_count += 1
        print(f"[+] Indexed {asset_count} binary assets from {label}")

        code_count = 0
        for lst_file in root.glob("*.lst"):
            bin_candidate = lst_file.with_suffix(".bin")
            if not bin_candidate.exists():
                continue
            ref_rom = bin_candidate.read_bytes()
            routines = parse_as_listing(lst_file)
            for sym_name, start, size in routines:
                if start + size > len(ref_rom):
                    continue
                chunk = ref_rom[start : start + size]
                masked = mask_m68k_relocations(chunk)
                meta = {"type": "code", "repo": label, "name": sym_name, "ref_offset": f"0x{start:06X}", "size": size}
                code_exact_index.setdefault(chunk[:16], []).append((chunk, meta))
                code_masked_index.setdefault(masked[:16], []).append((masked, meta))
                code_count += 1
        if code_count > 0:
            print(f"[+] Indexed {code_count} compiled 68K routines from {label}")

    return asset_prefix_index, code_exact_index, code_masked_index

def scan_rom(rom_path, asset_idx, code_exact_idx, code_masked_idx):
    rom_data = Path(rom_path).read_bytes()
    rom_size = len(rom_data)
    print(f"[*] Scanning {rom_path} ({rom_size:,} bytes / {rom_size // BANK_SIZE} banks)...")

    matches = []
    pos = 0
    while pos <= rom_size - MIN_CODE_SIZE:
        matched = False

        if pos <= rom_size - MIN_ASSET_SIZE:
            p32 = rom_data[pos : pos + 32]
            if p32 in asset_idx:
                for ref_bytes, meta in asset_idx[p32]:
                    end_pos = pos + meta["size"]
                    if end_pos <= rom_size and rom_data[pos:end_pos] == ref_bytes:
                        matches.append({
                            "rom_offset": f"0x{pos:06X}",
                            "rom_offset_int": pos,
                            "bank": pos // BANK_SIZE,
                            "bank_offset": f"0x{pos % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
                            "size": meta["size"],
                            "kind": "asset",
                            "match_mode": "exact",
                            "repo": meta["repo"],
                            "symbol": meta["name"],
                        })
                        pos = (end_pos + 1) & ~1
                        matched = True
                        break
        if matched:
            continue

        p16 = rom_data[pos : pos + 16]
        if p16 in code_exact_idx:
            for ref_bytes, meta in code_exact_idx[p16]:
                end_pos = pos + meta["size"]
                if end_pos <= rom_size and rom_data[pos:end_pos] == ref_bytes:
                    matches.append({
                        "rom_offset": f"0x{pos:06X}",
                        "rom_offset_int": pos,
                        "bank": pos // BANK_SIZE,
                        "bank_offset": f"0x{pos % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
                        "size": meta["size"],
                        "kind": "code",
                        "match_mode": "exact",
                        "repo": meta["repo"],
                        "symbol": meta["name"],
                    })
                    pos = (end_pos + 1) & ~1
                    matched = True
                    break
        if matched:
            continue

        if code_masked_idx:
            window_masked = mask_m68k_relocations(rom_data[pos : pos + 16])
            if window_masked in code_masked_idx:
                for ref_masked, meta in code_masked_idx[window_masked]:
                    end_pos = pos + meta["size"]
                    if end_pos <= rom_size:
                        cand_masked = mask_m68k_relocations(rom_data[pos:end_pos])
                        if cand_masked == ref_masked:
                            matches.append({
                                "rom_offset": f"0x{pos:06X}",
                                "rom_offset_int": pos,
                                "bank": pos // BANK_SIZE,
                                "bank_offset": f"0x{pos % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
                                "size": meta["size"],
                                "kind": "code",
                                "match_mode": "relocated",
                                "repo": meta["repo"],
                                "symbol": meta["name"],
                            })
                            pos = (end_pos + 1) & ~1
                            matched = True
                            break
        if matched:
            continue

        pos += 2

    return matches

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Match upstream assets & 68K code against 40Mb ROM")
    parser.add_argument("--rom", default="Sonic_Delta_Origins_2026.bin", help="Path to 40Mbit ROM")
    parser.add_argument("--s1", default="s1disasm-AS", help="Path to s1disasm-AS")
    parser.add_argument("--s2", default="s2disasm-master", help="Path to s2disasm-master")
    parser.add_argument("--sk", default="skdisasm-master", help="Path to skdisasm-master")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default="upstream_matches.json", help="Output JSON report")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    repos = {"s1disasm": args.s1, "s2disasm": args.s2, "skdisasm": args.sk}
    asset_idx, code_exact_idx, code_masked_idx = index_reference_repos(repos)
    matches = scan_rom(args.rom, asset_idx, code_exact_idx, code_masked_idx)

    bank_summary = {}
    for m in matches:
        b = m["bank"]
        bank_summary[b] = bank_summary.get(b, 0) + m["size"]

    report = {
        "rom": args.rom,
        "total_matches": len(matches),
        "asset_matches": sum(1 for m in matches if m["kind"] == "asset"),
        "code_matches": sum(1 for m in matches if m["kind"] == "code"),
        "matched_bytes_per_bank": {f"bank_{k:02d}": v for k, v in sorted(bank_summary.items())},
        "matches": matches,
    }

    Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[+] Saved {len(matches)} total matches ({report['asset_matches']} assets, {report['code_matches']} code) -> {args.out}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
Use code with caution.

yes
Here are File 4 (collect_trace.py v2) and File 5 (blastem_trace_ssf2.gdb) for capturing and parsing live SSF2 bank switches in BlastEm (as well as MAME and Mesen).
File 4: collect_trace.py
Parses trace_banks.txt, deduplicates repeated bank writes, maps each Program Counter (PC) to its physical SSF2 bank and target ROM offset, and outputs resolved_switches.json.
python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import re
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

BANK_SIZE = 0x80000

SLOT_WINDOWS = {
    "SSF2_SRAM_CTRL": ("SRAM/ROM Control", 0x200000),
    "SSF2_BANK_1":    ("Slot 1", 0x080000),
    "SSF2_BANK_2":    ("Slot 2", 0x100000),
    "SSF2_BANK_3":    ("Slot 3", 0x180000),
    "SSF2_BANK_4":    ("Slot 4", 0x200000),
    "SSF2_BANK_5":    ("Slot 5", 0x280000),
    "SSF2_BANK_6":    ("Slot 6", 0x300000),
    "SSF2_BANK_7":    ("Slot 7", 0x380000),
}

TRACE_LINE_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\s*0x([0-9A-Fa-f]+)\s*\|\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)\s*\|\s*0x([0-9A-Fa-f]+)\s*(?:\|\s*(.*))?$"
)

def parse_trace_file(trace_path: Path):
    if not trace_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Trace log not found: {trace_path}")

    events = []
    seen_pairs = {}

    for raw_line in trace_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines():
        line = raw_line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#") or line.startswith("Hardware watchpoint"):
            continue

        m = TRACE_LINE_RE.match(line)
        if not m:
            continue

        pc_val = int(m.group(1), 16) & 0xFFFFFF
        reg_name = m.group(2).strip()
        bank_val = int(m.group(3), 16) & 0xFF
        note = (m.group(4) or "").strip()

        key = (pc_val, reg_name, bank_val)
        seen_pairs[key] = seen_pairs.get(key, 0) + 1

        if seen_pairs[key] == 1:
            slot_label, base_vaddr = SLOT_WINDOWS.get(reg_name, ("Unknown Slot", 0))
            target_rom_offset = f"0x{bank_val * BANK_SIZE:06X}" if reg_name != "SSF2_SRAM_CTRL" else None

            events.append({
                "pc": f"0x{pc_val:06X}",
                "pc_int": pc_val,
                "pc_bank": pc_val // BANK_SIZE,
                "pc_bank_offset": f"0x{pc_val % BANK_SIZE:05X}",
                "register": reg_name,
                "slot": slot_label,
                "mapped_window_start": f"0x{base_vaddr:06X}",
                "value_written": f"0x{bank_val:02X}",
                "value_int": bank_val,
                "target_rom_offset": target_rom_offset,
                "note": note,
                "hit_count": 1,
            })

    for ev in events:
        key = (ev["pc_int"], ev["register"], ev["value_int"])
        ev["hit_count"] = seen_pairs[key]

    return events

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Parse MAME/Mesen/BlastEm SSF2 trace logs")
    parser.add_argument("--trace", default="trace_banks.txt", help="Input trace log path")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default="resolved_switches.json", help="Output JSON path")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    events = parse_trace_file(Path(args.trace))
    slot_summary = {}
    for ev in events:
        slot_summary.setdefault(ev["register"], set()).add(ev["value_written"])

    report = {
        "source_trace": args.trace,
        "unique_switch_sites": len(events),
        "total_logged_writes": sum(e["hit_count"] for e in events),
        "observed_values_per_register": {k: sorted(v) for k, v in sorted(slot_summary.items())},
        "switches": events,
    }

    Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[+] Parsed {report['total_logged_writes']} SSF2 writes across {len(events)} unique PCs -> {args.out}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
Use code with caution.

Ready for File 6 (ghidra_auto_bank.py v3) and File 7 (split_to_repo.py v2) next?


yes
Here are File 6 (ghidra_auto_bank.py v3) and File 7 (split_to_repo.py v2) for Ghidra headless symbol/RAM/trace importing and modular symbol-boundary repository splitting.
File 6: ghidra_auto_bank.py
Imports hardware registers, upstream Work RAM symbols, matched routines/assets, Neto's custom hooks, and live runtime switch sites into Ghidra while sealing binary assets so compressed art is never mis-disassembled as code.
python
# ghidra_auto_bank.py (v3 + Runtime Switch Importer)
import os
import json
from ghidra.program.model.symbol import SourceType
from ghidra.program.model.data import ByteDataType, ArrayDataType
from ghidra.app.cmd.disassemble import DisassembleCommand

def sanitize_symbol(name):
    clean = "".join(c if c.isalnum() else "_" for c in name)
    while "__" in clean:
        clean = clean.replace("__", "_")
    return clean.strip("_")

def label_genesis_hardware():
    hw_regs = {
        0x00A10001: "HW_VERSION",
        0x00A10003: "JOYPAD_1_DATA",
        0x00A10005: "JOYPAD_2_DATA",
        0x00A10009: "JOYPAD_1_CTRL",
        0x00A1000B: "JOYPAD_2_CTRL",
        0x00A11100: "Z80_BUSREQ",
        0x00A11200: "Z80_RESET",
        0x00A130F1: "SSF2_SRAM_CTRL",
        0x00A130F3: "SSF2_BANK_1",
        0x00A130F5: "SSF2_BANK_2",
        0x00A130F7: "SSF2_BANK_3",
        0x00A130F9: "SSF2_BANK_4",
        0x00A130FB: "SSF2_BANK_5",
        0x00A130FD: "SSF2_BANK_6",
        0x00A130FF: "SSF2_BANK_7",
        0x00C00000: "VDP_DATA",
        0x00C00004: "VDP_CTRL",
        0x00C00008: "VDP_HV_COUNTER",
        0x00C00011: "PSG_CTRL",
    }
    sym_table = currentProgram.getSymbolTable()
    space = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
    for addr_int, name in hw_regs.items():
        try:
            sym_table.createLabel(space.getAddress(addr_int), name, SourceType.USER_DEFINED)
        except Exception:
            pass

def apply_ram_symbols():
    ram_path = "upstream_ram_symbols.json"
    if not os.path.exists(ram_path):
        return
    with open(ram_path, "r") as f:
        ram_map = json.load(f)
    sym_table = currentProgram.getSymbolTable()
    space = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
    for _, info in ram_map.items():
        addr = space.getAddress(info["address_int"])
        names = info.get("names", [])
        if names:
            primary_name = sanitize_symbol("_or_".join(names[:3]))
            try:
                sym_table.createLabel(addr, primary_name, SourceType.USER_DEFINED)
            except Exception:
                pass

def apply_upstream_matches(bank_index):
    matches_path = "upstream_matches.json"
    if not os.path.exists(matches_path):
        return
    with open(matches_path, "r") as f:
        report = json.load(f)
    sym_table = currentProgram.getSymbolTable()
    listing = currentProgram.getListing()
    space = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
    base_addr = currentProgram.getImageBase().getOffset()

    for m in report.get("matches", []):
        if m.get("bank") != bank_index:
            continue
        local_offset = int(m["bank_offset"], 16)
        size = m["size"]
        kind = m["kind"]
        repo = m["repo"]
        sym_raw = m["symbol"]
        mode = m.get("match_mode", "exact")
        target_addr = space.getAddress(base_addr + local_offset)
        sym_name = "{}_{}_{:05X}".format(repo, sanitize_symbol(sym_raw), local_offset)
        try:
            sym_table.createLabel(target_addr, sym_name, SourceType.USER_DEFINED)
            listing.setComment(target_addr, 0, "[UPSTREAM: {} | {} | {} | {} bytes]".format(repo, kind, mode, size))
        except Exception:
            pass

        if kind == "asset":
            end_addr = space.getAddress(base_addr + local_offset + size - 1)
            listing.clearCodeUnits(target_addr, end_addr, False)
            try:
                listing.createData(target_addr, ArrayDataType(ByteDataType.dataType, size, 1))
            except Exception:
                pass
        elif kind == "code":
            DisassembleCommand(target_addr, None, True).applyTo(currentProgram)

def apply_neto_hooks(bank_index):
    hooks_path = "neto_custom_hooks.json"
    if not os.path.exists(hooks_path):
        return
    with open(hooks_path, "r") as f:
        report = json.load(f)
    sym_table = currentProgram.getSymbolTable()
    listing = currentProgram.getListing()
    space = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
    base_addr = currentProgram.getImageBase().getOffset()

    for h in report.get("ssf2_accesses", []):
        if h.get("bank") != bank_index:
            continue
        local_offset = int(h["bank_offset"], 16)
        target_addr = space.getAddress(base_addr + local_offset)
        lbl = "Neto_SSF2_{}_{:05X}".format(h["register_name"], local_offset)
        try:
            sym_table.createLabel(target_addr, lbl, SourceType.USER_DEFINED)
            listing.setComment(target_addr, 0, "[NETO CUSTOM: {}]".format(h["disassembly"]))
            DisassembleCommand(target_addr, None, True).applyTo(currentProgram)
        except Exception:
            pass

def apply_runtime_switches(bank_index):
    switches_path = "resolved_switches.json"
    if not os.path.exists(switches_path):
        return
    with open(switches_path, "r") as f:
        report = json.load(f)
    sym_table = currentProgram.getSymbolTable()
    listing = currentProgram.getListing()
    space = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
    base_addr = currentProgram.getImageBase().getOffset()

    by_pc = {}
    for sw in report.get("switches", []):
        if sw.get("pc_bank") == bank_index:
            by_pc.setdefault(int(sw["pc_bank_offset"], 16), []).append(sw)

    for local_off, sw_list in sorted(by_pc.items()):
        target_addr = space.getAddress(base_addr + local_off)
        regs = sorted(set(s["register"] for s in sw_list))
        vals = sorted(set(s["value_written"] for s in sw_list))
        lbl = "Runtime_SSF2_{}_{:05X}".format("_".join(regs), local_off)
        comment = "[RUNTIME TRACE: {} <- {} ({} hits)]".format(",".join(regs), ",".join(vals), sum(s["hit_count"] for s in sw_list))
        try:
            sym_table.createLabel(target_addr, lbl, SourceType.USER_DEFINED)
            listing.setComment(target_addr, 0, comment)
        except Exception:
            pass

def run():
    prog_name = currentProgram.getName()
    bank_index = 0
    for part in prog_name.replace(".", "_").split("_"):
        if part.isdigit():
            bank_index = int(part)
            break
    label_genesis_hardware()
    apply_ram_symbols()
    apply_upstream_matches(bank_index)
    apply_neto_hooks(bank_index)
    apply_runtime_switches(bank_index)

run()
Use code with caution.

File 7: split_to_repo.py
Carves matched binary assets into assets/ and slices bank .asm files at symbol boundaries into src/s1disasm/, src/s2disasm/, src/skdisasm/, src/custom_neto/, and src/banks/.
python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import re
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

BANK_SIZE = 0x80000
LABEL_LINE_RE = re.compile(r"^([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\s*:")

def sanitize_label(path_str):
    stem = Path(path_str).stem
    clean = "".join(c if c.isalnum() else "_" for c in stem)
    while "__" in clean:
        clean = clean.replace("__", "_")
    return clean.strip("_")

def classify_symbol(label_name):
    for prefix in ("s1disasm", "s2disasm", "skdisasm"):
        if label_name.startswith(prefix + "_"):
            return prefix
    if label_name.startswith("Neto_"):
        return "custom_neto"
    return None

def slice_bank_asm_by_symbols(bank_file, out_root, master_includes):
    bank_stem = bank_file.stem
    lines = bank_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines()

    segments = []
    cur_folder = "banks"
    cur_name = f"{bank_stem}_header"
    cur_lines = []

    for line in lines:
        m = LABEL_LINE_RE.match(line.strip())
        if m:
            lbl = m.group(1)
            folder = classify_symbol(lbl)
            if folder:
                if cur_lines:
                    segments.append((cur_folder, cur_name, cur_lines))
                cur_folder = folder
                cur_name = lbl
                cur_lines = [line]
                continue
        cur_lines.append(line)

    if cur_lines:
        segments.append((cur_folder, cur_name, cur_lines))

    for idx, (folder, mod_name, seg_lines) in enumerate(segments):
        if folder == "banks":
            rel_path = Path("src") / "banks" / f"{bank_stem}_part{idx:03d}.asm"
        else:
            rel_path = Path("src") / folder / f"{mod_name}.asm"

        dest = out_root / rel_path
        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        dest.write_text("\n".join(seg_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        master_includes.append(f'    include "{rel_path.as_posix()}"')

def build_repo_structure(rom_path, matches_json, hooks_json, asm_dir, out_repo):
    rom_data = Path(rom_path).read_bytes()
    report = json.loads(Path(matches_json).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    matches = report.get("matches", [])

    out = Path(out_repo)
    for sub in ("src/banks", "src/custom_neto", "src/s1disasm", "src/s2disasm", "src/skdisasm", "assets"):
        (out / sub).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    asset_inc_lines = ["; Auto-generated Upstream Asset Table", ""]
    asset_count = 0
    for m in matches:
        if m.get("kind") != "asset":
            continue
        start = m["rom_offset_int"]
        size = m["size"]
        repo_tag = m["repo"]
        rel_upstream = Path(m["symbol"])

        dest_file = out / "assets" / repo_tag / rel_upstream
        dest_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        dest_file.write_bytes(rom_data[start : start + size])

        lbl = f"{repo_tag}_{sanitize_label(str(rel_upstream))}_{start:06X}"
        rel_inc = dest_file.relative_to(out).as_posix()
        asset_inc_lines.append(f"{lbl}:")
        asset_inc_lines.append(f'    incbin "{rel_inc}"')
        asset_inc_lines.append("")
        asset_count += 1

    (out / "src" / "upstream_assets.asm").write_text("\n".join(asset_inc_lines), encoding="utf-8")

    mapper_inc = """SRAM_CTRL       equ $A130F1
BANK_REG_1      equ $A130F3
BANK_REG_2      equ $A130F5
BANK_REG_3      equ $A130F7
BANK_REG_4      equ $A130F9
BANK_REG_5      equ $A130FB
BANK_REG_6      equ $A130FD
BANK_REG_7      equ $A130FF

SET_BANK macro reg, bank_id
    move.b  #\\bank_id, (\\reg).l
    endm
"""
    (out / "src" / "ssf2_mapper.inc").write_text(mapper_inc, encoding="utf-8")

    neto_inc_local = Path("src/custom_neto/neto_hooks_map.inc")
    has_neto_inc = False
    if neto_inc_local.exists():
        (out / "src" / "custom_neto" / "neto_hooks_map.inc").write_text(
            neto_inc_local.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        has_neto_inc = True

    master_lines = [
        '    include "src/ssf2_mapper.inc"',
        '    include "src/upstream_assets.asm"',
    ]
    if has_neto_inc:
        master_lines.append('    include "src/custom_neto/neto_hooks_map.inc"')
    master_lines.append("")

    asm_source = Path(asm_dir)
    bank_files = sorted(asm_source.glob("bank_*.asm")) if asm_source.exists() else []
    for bf in bank_files:
        slice_bank_asm_by_symbols(bf, out, master_lines)

    (out / "main.asm").write_text("\n".join(master_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    makefile_text = f"""VASM = vasmm68k_mot
VASMFLAGS = -Fbin -spaces -devpac
TARGET = build/sonic_delta_40mb_rebuilt.bin
ORIG_ROM = {rom_path}

all: $(TARGET) verify

$(TARGET): main.asm
        @mkdir -p build
        $(VASM) $(VASMFLAGS) -o $(TARGET) main.asm

verify: $(TARGET)
        @cmp -l $(ORIG_ROM) $(TARGET) | head -n 20 || true
        @sha256sum $(ORIG_ROM) $(TARGET)

clean:
        rm -rf build
"""
    (out / "Makefile").write_text(makefile_text, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[+] Generated repository at {out.resolve()} ({asset_count} assets, {len(bank_files)} banks)")

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Build modular Git repo from 40Mb ROM & matches")
    parser.add_argument("--rom", default="Sonic_Delta_Origins_2026.bin")
    parser.add_argument("--matches", default="upstream_matches.json")
    parser.add_argument("--hooks", default="neto_custom_hooks.json")
    parser.add_argument("--asm-dir", default="disasm_banks")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default="sonic_delta_disasm_repo")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    build_repo_structure(args.rom, args.matches, args.hooks, args.asm_dir, args.out)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
